# Claims ledger

This ledger tracks propositions separately from sources. “Verified” means verified as a record or statement—not necessarily that a subjective or autobiographical claim is objectively proven.

| ID | Claim | Status | Supporting / contesting sources | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|

The linked MP4 returned HTTP 403 requiring SharePoint authentication; no transcript or captions were recovered. |

The childhood narrative is publisher/memoir framing, not independent corroboration; it should remain versioned alongside the US publisher, Google Books, trade-review, and first-person records. |
